Method: OmniOpt包含四个耦合组件：(1) 五阶段元流水线，将每个优化器更新视为结构化变换；(2) 使用范数约束的线性最小化预言机统一不同优化器；(3) 双维度分类法，一维按机制家族归类，另一维记录其旨在改善的可测量训练目标；(4) 跨领域基准测试，覆盖代表性优化器、模型规模和训练范式（从语言模型预训练到图像分类）。

Result: 通过跨领域基准测试，系统分析了各机制家族在多个效果目标上的表现，揭示了不同优化器的权衡关系，为研究社区提供了可操作的优化器选择坐标系。

Conclusion: OmniOpt为研究社区提供了一个基于显式机制和目标假设的优化器选择操作坐标系，并为优化器社区的未来发展指明了方向。

Abstract: Optimizer selection for large-scale model training has become a system-level design decision constrained jointly by compute, memory, tuning budget, and task diversity, yet the landscape of over one hundred methods remains fragmented. We therefore present OmniOpt, a unified survey and benchmark cookbook of optimizers for the research community. OmniOpt rests on four coupled components. First, we treat every optimizer update as a structured transformation through a five-stage meta-pipeline, and show that most methods engage only one or two of these stages. Second, we use norm-constrained linear minimization oracles (LMOs) to unify different optimizers. Third, these two views ground a dual-dimension taxonomy, one dimension assigning each method to a mechanism family and the other recording the measurable training objectives it aims to improve. Fourth, and at the core of this paper, we instantiate the full taxonomy in a unified cross-domain benchmark spanning representative optimizers, model scales, and training regimes from language model pretraining to image classification, systematically analyzing each method family across multiple effect objectives and laying out their trade-offs. OmniOpt thus supplies the research community with an operational coordinate system for selecting optimizers under explicit mechanism and objective assumptions, and charts a direction for the future development of the optimizer community.

</details>


### [222] [Reward-Gated On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04037)
*Mohammad Sadegh Akhondzadeh,Vijay Lingam,Atula Tejaswi,Chanakya Ekbote,Sujay Sanghavi,Aleksandar Bojchevski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RG-OPD（奖励门控在线策略蒸馏），通过验证器反馈决定何时信任教师logits，以解决传统在线策略蒸馏中教师监督不可靠的问题，在推理和代码基准测试上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的在线策略蒸馏中，教师模型在学生采样的轨迹上提供密集的token级监督，但这种监督并不可靠：教师可能会对看似合理但错误的解决方案赋予高概率，或对学生正确的但推理路径不同的方案赋予低概率。无差别地蒸馏教师会强化不良模式或消除有用的学生行为。

Method: RG-OPD引入验证器（verifier）反馈作为门控机制，决定何时该信任教师logits。该方法桥接了稀疏的验证器奖励和密集的教师logits，在保留token级监督的同时过滤掉误导性的教师信号。

Result: 在推理和代码基准测试中，RG-OPD优于原始的reverse-KL蒸馏和最新的TSD-KD基线。在1K生成长度下，比reverse-KL提升2.9个点，比TSD-KD提升4.9个点；在长文本生成场景下，比未调优的学生提升8.2个点。

Conclusion: RG-OPD通过奖励门控机制有效解决了在线策略蒸馏中教师监督不可靠的问题，在多项基准上取得了显著改进，代码已开源。

Abstract: On-policy distillation is a powerful way to transfer reasoning ability from a strong teacher to a smaller student: the student samples trajectories from its own policy, and the teacher provides dense token-level supervision on the states the student actually visits. However, this supervision is not always reliable: a teacher can assign high likelihood to plausible but incorrect solutions, or low likelihood to correct student solutions that follow different reasoning paths. Unconditionally distilling the teacher can therefore reinforce bad modes or erase useful student behavior. To address these limitations, we introduce RG-OPD: Reward-Gated On-Policy Distillation that uses verifier feedback to decide when teacher logits should be trusted. RG-OPD bridges sparse verifier rewards and dense teacher logits, preserving token-level supervision while filtering misleading teacher signals. Across reasoning and coding benchmarks, RG-OPD produces stronger distilled students, outperforming both vanilla reverse-KL distillation and the recent TSD-KD baseline. At 1K generation length, RG-OPD improves over reverse-KL by 2.9 points and over TSD-KD by 4.9 points; in the long-generation setting, it improves over the untuned student by 8.2 points. Our code is available at https://github.com/UoC-tail/RG-OPD.

</details>


### [223] [A Unified Framework for In-Context Learning with Causal and Masked Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04081)
*Chenrui Liu,Chuanlong Xie,Falong Tan,Yicheng Zeng,Lixing Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个统计学习框架，统一分析掩码语言模型和自回归语言模型在上下文学习中的理论表现，证明了在 Wasserstein 正则性条件下两者具有相同阶数的上界，并通过实验验证掩码模型能达到与因果Transformer相当的ICL性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICL的理论分析主要集中于因果语言模型（如GPT），而掩码语言模型在ICL中的作用尚不明确，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解两种预训练目标下的ICL行为。

Method: 构建基于经验测度的统计学习框架，将自回归和掩码预训练目标纳入统一的风险分析；利用Wasserstein距离处理任务分布偏移；在预训练数据预算约束下推导最优分配策略。

Result: 掩码和自回归目标下k-shot超额风险上界同阶；任务分布偏移时上界增加由提升Wasserstein距离控制的附加项；推导出预训练数据预算约束下的阶最优分配和内在低维结构下的改进速率；Masked Pair Encoder性能与GPT-2相当。

Conclusion: ICL行为并非因果语言模型所特有，掩码语言模型也能实现类似的上下文学习能力，为理解不同预训练范式下的ICL提供了统一的理论基础。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) has emerged as a central capability of pretrained language models, yet its theoretical analysis has focused primarily on causal language models trained by left-to-right autoregressive prediction, such as GPT-style models. Masked language models instead recover masked tokens from bidirectional context, and their role in ICL remains less understood. We develop a statistical learning framework that represents the context examples by their empirical measure and models prediction as a function of the context and the query. This formulation places autoregressive and masked pretraining objectives within a common excess-risk analysis. Under Wasserstein-type regularity conditions, we relate pretraining with T tasks and N samples per task to k-shot excess risk at inference, obtaining same-order upper bounds for masked and autoregressive objectives. We also study task-distribution shift, where pretraining tasks are sampled from P and inference tasks from Q; the resulting bound contains an additional term controlled by the lifted Wasserstein distance between P and Q. The bounds further imply an order-optimal allocation under a fixed pretraining data budget and refined rates under intrinsic low-dimensional structure. Experiments on controlled function-learning tasks show that the Masked Pair Encoder (MPE) can achieve performance comparable to GPT-2-style causal Transformers, suggesting that ICL behavior is not specific to causal language models.

</details>


### [224] [FedSPM: Routing-Enabled Federated Learning under Dual Heterogeneity via Semiparametric Mixture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04085)
*Zijian Wang,Pengfei Li,Guangyu Yang,Qiong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedSPM框架，通过半参数混合模型解决联邦学习中客户端内外双重异质性问题，在路由和预测任务上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由预测联邦学习方法假设客户端内部是同质的，忽略了客户端内部存在的潜在子群（如同一医院中不同亚型的患者）。这种客户端间与客户端内异质性并存（称为双重异质性）会损害路由和预测性能。

Method: 提出FedSPM，一个支持路由的半参数混合框架。每个客户端由多个客户端特定的潜在成分表示，每个成分结合分类预测分布和路由特征分布。通过经验似然估计相对于公共非参数度量的密度比来灵活建模特征分布并跨客户端共享信息。开发了联邦EM算法优化近似代理目标，并证明了在代理误差受控时精确剖面目标以O(1/√T)速率收敛。

Result: 在受控基准和真实医学数据上的实验表明，FedSPM在双重异质性下的一致改善了路由和预测性能。

Conclusion: FedSPM有效解决了双重异质性问题，为联邦学习中的路由预测提供了更精细的建模能力，且具有理论收敛保证。

Abstract: Routing-prediction federated learning has emerged as a new paradigm that reframes inter-client heterogeneity as a resource for system-level intelligence: at inference time, the server routes each external query to the best-matched client for prediction. Existing approaches, however, typically treat each client as internally homogeneous, overlooking latent subpopulations within local data. For example, patients with the same diagnosis at one hospital may exhibit morphologically distinct disease subtypes. The coexistence of inter-client and intra-client heterogeneity, which we call dual heterogeneity, can impair both routing and prediction. To address this challenge, we propose FedSPM, a routing-enabled semiparametric mixture framework that represents each client using client-specific latent components. Each component combines a predictive distribution for classification with a feature distribution for routing. To flexibly model feature distributions while effectively sharing information across clients, FedSPM models their density ratios relative to a common nonparametric measure estimated via empirical likelihood. We develop a federated expectation-maximization algorithm that optimizes a tractable surrogate and prove convergence of the exact profiled objective at the standard $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ rate when the surrogate errors are properly controlled. Experiments on controlled benchmarks and real-world medical data demonstrate consistent improvements in routing and prediction under dual heterogeneity. Code is available at https://github.com/zijianwang0510/FedSPM.

</details>


### [225] [Target-Aware Interaction-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Black-Box Node Injection Attacks on Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04091)
*Yi Lan,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TIRBA，一种面向GNN黑盒节点注入攻击的目标感知交互引导强化学习方法，将攻击建模为马尔可夫决策过程，在异构动作空间中联合优化恶意节点特征生成与边连接，通过目标感知交互编码器、类中心引导机制和拓扑差异感知状态值评估，实现了优于现有方法的攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒节点注入攻击方法通常将恶意节点特征生成与边连接解耦，导致在严格预算下攻击效果次优，亟需一种联合优化的攻击方法以提升攻击效能。

Method: TIRBA将节点注入攻击建模为马尔可夫决策过程，在异构动作空间中联合优化特征生成与边构建；设计目标感知交互编码器融合节点特征与边信息；引入类中心引导机制利用先验类别分布信息指导高维特征空间探索；采用拓扑差异感知状态值评估捕获局部结构异常以稳定强化学习训练。

Result: 实验结果表明，TIRBA显著优于当前最先进的黑盒节点注入攻击方法。

Conclusion: TIRBA通过强化学习框架联合优化节点特征与边结构，有效提升了黑盒节点注入攻击在严格预算下的攻击效果，为GNN安全性研究提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have achieved remarkable performance in graph representation learning, yet their inherent vulnerability to adversarial attacks poses severe security risks. Especially, black-box node injection attacks have become a major threat to GNNs since they inject malicious nodes without altering the original graph topology. However, they typically decouple the generation of malicious node features and edge connections, thereby resulting in suboptimal attack efficacy under stringent budgets. To address this critical issue, this study proposes a novel Target-aware Interaction-guided Reinforcement learning for Black-box node injection Attacks on GNNs (TIRBA), which formulates the attack as a Markov Decision Process and jointly optimizes node feature generation and edge construction in a heterogeneous action space. Firstly, TIRBA designs a target-aware interaction encoder to fuse information of node features and edges. Further, it introduces a class-center guidance mechanism to utilize prior class distribution information, thereby guiding efficient exploration of the high-dimensional feature space. Finally, a topology difference-aware state value evaluation is adopted to explicitly capture local structural anomalies caused by injected nodes, thereby stabilizing the reinforcement learning training process. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed TIRBA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art black-box node injection attack methods.

</details>


### [226] [Dictionaries, Not Darwin: Set-Level Selection Beats LLM Evolution in Scientific Equation Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04108)
*Pan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过审计发现，在科学方程发现中，基于LLM的迭代进化循环（父代条件生成）与独立随机采样效果无显著差异，并将问题诊断归因于数据欠定性导致单项归因不可靠。为此提出PTB-Search方法：仅需一轮LLM采样，构建候选项词典，通过训练集级别的集合选择（而非单项选择）进行稀疏回归。实验表明集合选择器比单项选择器解决更多问题（165-169 vs 74-78），在LLM-SRBench基准上以十分之一的调用预算达到73.2%-77.0%的Acc0.1，远超基线49.2%。结论：LLM应被视为材料供应商，发现能力来自外部的集合级选择机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑并审计当前流行的LLM迭代进化范式在科学方程发现中的有效性，验证其是否真正带来复合性发现收益，并诊断其失效原因。

Method: PTB-Search：单轮LLM采样提取可复用术语构建词典，在训练集上使用最小二乘系数进行集合级稀疏选择；对比实验包括父代条件生成、多父代交叉等迭代方案。

Result: 1) 父代条件进化与独立采样无显著差异（OOD NMSE 0.045 vs 0.049）；2) 集合选择器解决165-169个问题，单项选择器仅74-78个；3) PTB-Search在LLM-SRBench上以十分之一调用预算达73.2%-77.0% Acc0.1，基线为49.2%。

Conclusion: LLM的最佳定位是材料供应商，科学发现的核心驱动力是外部集合级选择机制对可复用组件的筛选，而非迭代进化循环本身。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as evolutionary engines for scientific discovery: generate candidates, select winners, feed them back as parents, and repeat. We audit whether this loop actually compounds discovery in scientific equation discovery, a setting where finite samples make structure underdetermined and interpolation easy. Under matched LLM-call budgets, parent-conditioned evolution is indistinguishable from fresh independent sampling: median OOD NMSE is 0.045 vs. 0.049, instructed multi-parent crossover is worse, final success is predicted by initial proposal quality, and multiple iteration schemes fail to add solved problems. Operationally, the loop reduces to what it produces: a dictionary of candidate terms. We turn that diagnosis into PTB-Search, a one-generation method for componentized scientific discovery. PTB-Search samples independent LLM proposals once, extracts reusable terms into a per-problem dictionary, and performs train-only set-level sparse selection with least-squares coefficients. Its central principle is that underdetermined data identifies the joint behavior of term sets, not reliable per-term credit. On identical dictionaries and zero additional LLM calls, set-level selectors solve 165--169 of 717 cells, while single-term reductions solve only 74--78. On the official 239-problem LLM-SRBench split, PTB-Search reaches 73.2% Acc0.1 with Llama-3.1-8B and 77.0% with a single-seed DeepSeek-V4 anchor, versus 49.2% for the best reported baseline, using one tenth of the standardized call budget. A program-domain stress test gives a scoped boundary: generation count remains unreliable, while retained external state can help in harder non-linear spaces. Across these results, LLMs are best understood as material suppliers; discovery is carried by external set-level selection over reusable components.

</details>


### [227] [DynaVieW: Schema-Guided World Modeling for Understanding Hierarchical Visual Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04112)
*Silin Gao,Hao Zhao,Zeming Chen,Sepideh Mamooler,Antara Raaghavi Bhattacharya,Qiyu Wu,Hiromi Wakaki,Yuki Mitsufuji,Li Mi,Syrielle Montariol,Antoine Bosselut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DynaVieW，一种动态模式引导的世界模型，通过联合建模状态-转换序列和混合专家架构，增强多模态大语言模型对视觉场景时间演化的理解和模拟能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在处理视频或多图像序列时，难以系统性地建模视觉场景的时间演化过程，无法有效预测或模拟动态组成要素（如动作及其导致的环境变化）。

Method: 提出DynaVieW，利用层级模式学习交错的状态-转换序列（状态覆盖视频关键帧的视觉场景，转换捕获动态组成要素）；采用混合专家架构联合建模转换预测与状态模拟，引入跨专家选择性注意力和模式令牌重加权损失实现鲁棒学习。

Result: DynaVieW在视觉叙事生成和世界模拟等下游任务中表现提升，具有更好的一致性、可控性和指令跟随能力。

Conclusion: 通过动态模式引导的世界模型联合建模状态与转换，可有效提升多模态模型对视觉动态的理解与模拟能力，并带来下游性能增益。

Abstract: Multimodal LLMs struggle to systematically model the temporal evolution of visual scenes in videos or multi-image sequences. Such inputs require models to predict or simulate multiple levels of dynamic constituents, such as actions taken in the visual sequence, and the associated changes to the visual environment that result. To address this challenge, we propose a dynamic schema-guided world model, DynaVieW, optimized for visual dynamic prediction and simulation. DynaVieW achieves an in-depth understanding of visual dynamics by learning interleaved state-transition sequences, where states cover broad visual scenes from video keyframes, and transitions capture comprehensive dynamic constituents within a hierarchical schema. DynaVieW jointly models transition prediction and state simulation under a mixture-of-experts architecture, with a cross-expert selective attention and a schema token re-weighted loss, to ensure effective and robust learning. DynaVieW's understanding of visual dynamics boosts its downstream performance in visual narrative creation and world simulation, showing improved consistency, controllability, and instruction-following.

</details>


### [228] [Asymptotic-Preserving A Posteriori Analysis of Diffusion and Flow-Matching Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04113)
*Shiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散和流匹配采样器在奇异摄动参数σ_min趋近于0时的渐近保持（AP）性质。确定了固定步长采样器的AP准则，将其转化为可基于预训练检查点计算的后验审计框架。发现DDIM更新是唯一在终端层精确的离散化方法；确定性采样器具有一阶均匀精度且无log(1/σ_min)因子，而对数项完全由随机采样器的伊藤项承担。在EDM CIFAR-10检查点上验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流匹配模型在采样过程中，当噪声尺度σ_min趋近于0时，分数函数变得刚性并出现边界层，导致数值采样器不稳定或精度损失。本文旨在系统分析不同固定步长采样器在σ_min→0极限下的渐近性质，建立可基于预训练模型检查点计算的后验审计框架，无需真实分数或精确轨迹。

Method: 将σ_min视为奇异摄动参数，分析不同时间时钟（σ-clock、λ-clock、uniform-σ² heat clock）下采样器的稳定性和精度。在解析可解模型（秩亏高斯分布、对称两点混合分布）上验证理论，并在EDM CIFAR-10检查点上进行实验验证。

Result: 1) DDIM（σ-clock下的欧拉法）是唯一在终端层精确的离散化方法，整流流是其流匹配对应物。2) λ-clock的稳定性步长阈值h_⋆=1+W(1/e)。3) 确定性采样器具有一阶均匀精度，无log(1/σ_min)因子。4) 对数项完全由随机采样器的伊藤项承担，其路径KL尺度为Λ²/N，而ODE为O(Λ²/N²)。5) 在EDM CIFAR-10上，一次测量的频谱可预测不同步数、调度和噪声水平下的残差预算。

Conclusion: 时钟（时间参数化）决定稳定性，噪声（而非几何结构）承担对数因子。为扩散模型采样器的设计和后验质量评估提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching samplers integrate a learned probability-flow ODE from a large noise scale down to a small terminal floor $σ_{\min}$, at which the score is stiff and the flow develops a boundary layer. We treat $σ_{\min}$ as a singular-perturbation parameter and determine which fixed-step samplers are asymptotic-preserving (AP), that is, stable and uniformly accurate as $σ_{\min}\to0$, casting the criteria as an a posteriori audit: residual functionals with $σ_{\min}$-uniform coefficients, computable on a pretrained checkpoint without ground-truth scores or exact trajectories. On the terminal layer, Euler in the $σ$-clock, the deterministic DDIM update, is the unique layer-exact discretization up to affine reparameterization, with rectified flow its flow-matching counterpart; the $λ$-clock is stable only for steps $h\le h_\star=1+W(1/e)$, and the uniform-$σ^2$ heat clock stalls a $σ_{\min}$-independent distance from the data. On two solvable models (rank-deficient Gaussian, symmetric two-point mixture), deterministic samplers remain first-order uniformly accurate with no $\log(1/σ_{\min})$ factor, even across a symmetric posterior-switching interface whose distributional budget is a universal constant; the logarithm is charged entirely to the Itô term of stochastic samplers, whose path-KL scales as $Λ^2/N$ against the ODE's $O(Λ^2/N^2)$ budget, with $Λ=\log(σ_{\max}/σ_{\min})$. On the EDM CIFAR-10 checkpoint, spectra measured once predict held-out residual budgets across step count, schedule, and noise level against pre-specified gates with no per-configuration refitting, and calibrate the Itô coefficient at $M_1=1.00\pm0.01$. The clock decides stability; the noise, not the geometry, charges the logarithm.

</details>


### [229] [GlacierCastAI: Predicting Glacier Retreat from Multi-Modal Satellite Imagery and Climate Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04117)
*Arunkumar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GlacierCastAI，将冰川边界预测重构为多模态时空预测问题，融合多时相Landsat影像、ERA5再分析气候变量和DEM地形特征。结果显示加入ERA5气候信号使IoU从0.326提升至0.337（+3.4%），而轻量级气候MLP基线（66.1K参数）以85倍少的参数达到了影像模型98%的性能，SHAP分析表明春季太阳辐射是主导气候驱动因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法仅能映射当前冰川边界，无法预测未来变化，而ERA5季节气候变量包含超越卫星影像的预测信息。作者旨在将冰川边界预测从当前映射问题升级为多模态时空预测问题，探索气候数据对预测未来冰川退缩的增量价值。

Method: 将冰川边界预测重构为多模态时空预测问题，融合多时相Landsat遥感影像、ERA5再分析气候变量和Copernicus DEM地形特征。采用ResNet50空间编码器+ConvLSTM时间模型+交叉注意力气候融合模块的耦合架构，覆盖五个冰川（四种气候机制），并进行预注册消融实验和SHAP归因分析。

Result: 加入ERA5气候信号使图像-only IoU从0.326提升至0.337（+3.4%）；所有深度学习模型显著优于持续性基线（0.160）和线性趋势基线（0.169），相对IoU提升89-99%；轻量级气候MLP（66.1K参数）以85倍少的参数达到图像模型98%的性能（IoU 0.320）；SHAP分析表明春季太阳辐射是主导气候驱动因子。

Conclusion: 大气强迫（气候变量）携带超越遥感影像的增量预测信息；气候数据本身即蕴含可观的预测信号，为资源受限场景提供轻量级方案；春季太阳辐射是预测冰川边界的关键气候因子，与已知的春季日照对融化季节轨迹的影响机制一致。

Abstract: ERA5 seasonal climate variables contain predictive information about future glacier retreat beyond what satellite imagery alone provides, yet existing deep learning methods focus on mapping current boundaries rather than forecasting future ones. This paper presents GlacierCastAI, which reframes glacier boundary prediction as a multi-modal spatiotemporal forecasting problem, fusing multi-temporal Landsat imagery with ERA5 reanalysis climate variables and Copernicus DEM terrain features to forecast glacier boundaries across five glaciers spanning four climate regimes. The architecture couples a ResNet50 spatial encoder with a ConvLSTM temporal model and a cross-attention climate fusion module. Because forecasting is inherently more uncertain than mapping current boundaries, the reported IoU values (0.320-0.337) are not directly comparable to state-of-the-art mapping models. Comparisons are against traditional baselines and experimental conditions. Through a pre-registered ablation study, adding ERA5 climate signals improves image-only IoU from 0.326 to 0.337 (+3.4%), suggesting that atmospheric forcing carries predictive information beyond imagery alone. All deep learning models substantially outperform persistence and linear trend baselines (IoU 0.160 and 0.169 respectively), with improvements of 89-99% relative IoU. A lightweight climate-only MLP baseline (661K parameters) achieves an IoU of 0.320 (98% of image-only performance) using 85x fewer parameters, suggesting that ERA5 variables encode substantial predictive signal independently of satellite imagery. SHAP attribution analysis suggests that spring solar radiation (MAM) is the dominant climate driver, consistent with the known role of spring insolation in setting melt season trajectories.

</details>


### [230] [HiFA4: Training-Free 4-bit FlashAttention on Ascend HIF4 NPUs for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04302)
*Hui Dong,Yanzhao Li,Jie Gao,Chunlu Li,Zhiyuan Zhang,Yupeng Sun,Zhenyuan Chen,Zhiqiang Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiFA4是一种面向Ascend NPU的4位HIF4注意力算子设计，通过Smooth-QK和P-Reordering两种机制，在FlashAttention中实现4比特QK^T和PV矩阵乘，将Qwen3-8B上MMLU准确率损失从1.12 pp降至0.70 pp，不一致预测从16.3%降至8.2%，回归减少57%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM推理中，FlashAttention的高精度（如BF16）计算开销大，而直接使用4位低精度量化会导致显著的准确率下降（如Qwen3-8B上1.12 pp的准确率损失和16.3%的不一致预测），因此需要一种既能降低计算成本又能保持模型精度的后训练算子级设计。

Method: HiFA4包含两个核心机制：(1) Smooth-QK：在RoPE之后对Q和K进行校准静态的逐通道等效重缩放，将量化困难从K转移到Q，无需推理时逐块在线降尺度；(2) P-Reordering：使用与PV GEMM相同的量化注意力权重P_hat累加softmax归一化子，而非使用高精度重建，并将归一化子融合到PV Cube GEMM中。该方法会产生输出缩放误差，但经验验证该误差表现为净概率质量损失（中位数epsilon_bar=-0.064）。

Result: 在5个LLM上评估：(1) Qwen3-8B：恢复37.5%的量化精度差距，准确率损失从1.12 pp降至0.70 pp，不一致预测从16.3%降至8.2%，回归减少57%（1071→465）；(2) Gemma2-9B：保持0.7 pp内，回归减少27%；(3) LLaMA3.1-8B、Mistral-7B、Phi-4B（禁用Smooth-QK）：P-Reordering+Q-Mean辅助使回归减少41-52%。指令调度分析预估相较BF16关键路径延迟降低35.4%。

Conclusion: HiFA4通过精心设计的4比特注意力机制量化方案，在保持计算效率的同时有效缩小了与BF16基线的精度差距，验证了低精度注意力计算中不一致公式的可行性及其可校正的误差特性。软硬件协同设计有望实现实际加速，片上验证留待未来工作。

Abstract: We present HiFA4, a post-training operator-level design that executes both QK^T and PV in FlashAttention as 4-bit HIF4 Cube GEMMs for LLM inference on Ascend NPUs, while maintaining the online softmax state in FP16. To our knowledge, HiFA4 is the first Ascend-HIF4-targeted design of this kind evaluated on standard NLP benchmarks.
  HiFA4 combines two mechanisms. Smooth-QK applies a calibration-static per-channel equivalent rescaling to Q and K after RoPE, transferring quantization difficulty from K to Q without per-tile online reduction at inference. P-Reordering accumulates the softmax normalizer from the same quantized attention weights P_hat used in the PV GEMM, rather than from a higher-precision reconstruction. We show that this inconsistent formulation introduces a coherent output-scaling error, and validate the effect on a Qwen3-8B Layer-0 MMLU trace, where all 3.6M measured attention tiles exhibit net probability-mass loss with median epsilon_bar = -0.064. P-Reordering also allows the normalizer to be fused into the PV Cube GEMM.
  Across five LLMs, HiFA4 reduces quantization-induced decision drift. On Qwen3-8B, it recovers 37.5% of the accuracy gap introduced by direct HIF4 quantization, narrows the sample-weighted accuracy loss from 1.12 pp to 0.70 pp, reduces BF16-inconsistent MMLU predictions from 16.3% to 8.2%, and cuts MMLU accuracy regressions by 57% (1071 to 465). On Gemma2-9B, mild smoothing keeps HiFA4 within 0.7 pp of BF16 while reducing MMLU regressions by 27%. On LLaMA3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, and Phi-4B, where Smooth-QK is disabled, P-Reordering with the adopted Q-Mean auxiliary still reduces full-set MMLU regressions by 41-52%. A preliminary instruction-scheduling analysis projects a 35.4% critical-path latency reduction relative to BF16 by fusing the softmax normalizer into the PV Cube GEMM; on-hardware validation is left to future work.

</details>


### [231] [Parametric Memory Decoding for Zero-Shot Routing in LoRA-Based External Parametric Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04118)
*Fengxian Ji,Zhuohan Xie,Jingpu Yang,Fan Zhang,Zirui Song,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出参数记忆解码（PMD）框架，将零样本LoRA路由重构为激活解码问题，并基于该框架实例化PMDRouter方法，通过单次基模型预填充的响应幅度为每个LoRA打分，在构建的PMD-Bench基准上实现了无需额外路由组件的强零样本路由性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LoRA的外部参数记忆（EPM）路由方法会引入额外的训练、部署和维护开销，且现有零样本LoRA路由方法在EPM设定下存在两个问题：（1）评估分散在不同任务设定中，未围绕EPM访问组织；（2）路由信号缺乏统一视角来指导系统性改进。

Method: 提出参数记忆解码（PMD）框架，将零样本LoRA路由重新定义为在外部参数记忆上解码激活的过程；基于PMD实例化PMDRouter，通过单次基模型预填充，计算每个LoRA的响应幅度作为路由信号进行打分。

Result: 在包含文档级、领域级知识和任务技能三类场景的PMD-Bench基准上，PMDRouter在多个零样本路由设定下取得了最强的内部信号性能。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了零样本LoRA路由的可行性，PMD可作为通用框架用于改进零样本路由方法。

Abstract: With the rise of parametric memory, LoRA-based External Parametric Memory (EPM) has emerged as a modular solution, but existing routing methods often introduce additional training, deployment, and maintenance overhead. This raises a natural question: can a LoRA-based EPM bank be routed without maintaining an additional routing component? However, existing zero-shot LoRA routing methods still face two problems under the EPM setting: (1) their evaluations are scattered across different task settings rather than organized around EPM access, and (2) their routing signals lack a unified perspective to guide systematic improvement. To address these problems, we organize PMD-Bench, covering document-level, domain-level knowledge, and task-skill, and propose Parametric Memory Decoding (PMD), the first framework designed to systematically improve zero-shot LoRA routing by reframing it as decoding activations over external parametric memory. Based on PMD, we further instantiate PMDRouter, which scores each LoRA by its response magnitude from a single base-model prefill. Experiments on PMD-Bench show that PMDRouter achieves the strongest internal-signal performance across multiple zero-shot routing settings. These results demonstrate the feasibility of zero-shot LoRA routing and suggest that PMD can serve as a general framework for improving zero-shot routing methods. Sources: Github (https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Parametric-Memory-Decoding-872A/)

</details>


### [232] [URSA: Chemistry-Aware Benchmark for Utilitarian Retrosynthesis Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04688)
*Bogdan Zagribelnyy,Ivan Ilin,Nikita Bondarev,Anton Morgunov,Arkadii Lin,Maksim Kuznetsov,Rim Shayakhmetov,Vladimir Aladinskiy,Alex Aliper,Alex Zhavoronkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了URSA评估框架，用于从化学合理性和形式化两个维度对逆合成规划方法进行基准测试，发现专用逆合成模型在可靠性上优于通用大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆合成规划是药物发现中的关键任务，现有深度学习逆合成系统和通用大语言模型发展迅速，但缺乏灵活、可化学解释的基准测试协议来客观比较它们。

Method: 提出URSA评估框架，不仅从形式化角度（如是否收敛到商业可得起始物料）评估合成路线，还从化学合理性角度（模拟化学家评估反应和路线的方式）进行基准测试；对一组具有未公开合成路线的新型多样化目标分子进行全面评估。

Result: 大语言模型虽能支持高层次策略规划，但在可靠解决合成规划任务方面目前不如专用逆合成模型。

Conclusion: URSA框架为客观比较不同逆合成方法提供了更全面的基准；专用逆合成模型在实际药物设计任务中仍优于LLM。

Abstract: Synthesis planning aiming to find pathways of reactions for a target molecule is one of the most important and challenging tasks in drug discovery. Recent progress has produced both specialized deep-learning retrosynthesis systems and general-purpose large language models, but objective comparison remains difficult due to the lack of flexible, chemically interpretable benchmarking protocols. In the current study, we are introducing the URSA (Utilitarian RetroSynthesis Assessment) evaluation framework that provides the opportunity to benchmark the synthetic routes not only from a formal perspective, such as convergence to commercially available starting materials, but also from a chemical plausibility perspective, mimicking the way expert chemists evaluate the reactions and routes. The study covers a comprehensive evaluation of both conventional end-to-end retrosynthesis solutions and LLMs for the synthesis planning task on a set of novel, diverse target molecules with undisclosed synthetic routes, which represent realistic tasks in the daily drug design routine. We find that while LLMs can support high-level strategic planning, they currently underperform specialized retrosynthesis models in reliably solving synthesis planning tasks.

</details>


### [233] [CertMix: Certified, Data-Efficient Metamaterial Design by Affine Mixing of Aligned Neural-Implicit Weight Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04123)
*Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CertMix提出了一种数据高效的反向设计框架，将机械超材料的单元结构表示为周期神经隐式场（SIREN），利用对齐权重空间中线性的弹性张量特性，通过约束仿射混合实现目标设计，仅需50个样本即可达到10⁻⁴的缩放属性误差，性能比生成基线方法提升2-3个数量级，速度比拓扑优化快57倍，并支持外推、分布无证书和3D应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的机械超材料反向设计方法数据需求大、多为插值性质，且无法保证生成设计满足规定目标。

Method: 将每个示例单元结构表示为小型周期神经隐式场（SIREN符号距离解码器），使示例权重向量对齐且可直接比较；利用对齐权重空间中均匀化弹性张量近似线性的特性，将目标设计转化为约束仿射混合问题；通过可微周期均匀化器求解，使用负系数实现外推，线性失配信任区域保持混合有效性，分裂共形校准将失配信号转化为无分布证书。

Result: 仅需50个示例即可达到10⁻⁴的缩放属性误差，比条件生成基线（1000个单元）低2-3个数量级；远超出示例范围仍保持准确；比每目标拓扑优化快57倍且避免棋盘格和封闭空洞；可扩展到空间梯度场、3D三周期曲面和认证的跑鞋中底应用。

Conclusion: CertMix是一种数据高效、可外推、带保证的机械超材料反向设计方法，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Inverse design of mechanical metamaterials seeks a periodic unit cell whose homogenized elastic properties meet a prescribed target, but current learning-based methods are data-hungry, mostly interpolative, and provide no guarantee that the generated design satisfies the specification. We introduce CertMix, a data-efficient framework that represents each exemplar unit cell as a small periodic neural implicit field, specifically a SIREN signed-distance decoder overfit from a shared anchor, so that exemplar weight vectors become aligned and directly comparable. The key observation is that, in this aligned weight space, the homogenized elasticity tensor is approximately linear in the mixing coefficients. Targeted design therefore reduces to a small constrained affine-mixing problem solved with a differentiable periodic homogenizer in the loop. Negative coefficients enable extrapolation beyond the exemplar range, a linearity-mismatch trust region keeps blends valid, and split-conformal calibration converts the mismatch signal into a distribution-free certificate on achieved-property error. From as few as 50 exemplars, CertMix attains a scaled property error of $10^{-4}$, roughly two to three orders of magnitude below conditional generative baselines trained on 1000 cells. It remains accurate far outside the exemplar range, is $57\times$ faster than per-target topology optimization while avoiding checkerboards and enclosed voids, and extends to spatially graded fields, 3D triply periodic surfaces, and a certified running-shoe midsole application.

</details>


### [234] [Multi-Turn On-Policy Distillation with Prefix Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04763)
*Baohao Liao,Hanze Dong,Christof Monz,Xinxing Xu,Li Dong,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReOPD方法，通过重用预收集的教师轨迹作为重放前缀，在不进行环境交互的情况下实现多轮智能体任务的学生蒸馏，避免了在线策略蒸馏的高成本，同时解决了"前缀陷阱"问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在多轮智能体任务中成本高昂，因为每次更新都需要学生与环境交互并查询教师。作者希望找到一种更高效的替代方案，既能保留OPD的优势，又能大幅降低计算成本。

Method: 提出ReOPD（Replayed-Prefix On-Policy Distillation），重用预收集的教师轨迹作为重放前缀，学生在选定步骤行动，教师提供密集的逐步骤监督而无需新的环境交互。将多轮OPD视为可靠性感知的前缀分布设计问题，使用简单的步进衰减采样策略，强调早期、低偏移的前缀。

Result: 在数学推理和Python搜索环境中，跨多个教师和学生模型规模，ReOPD保持或提升了OPD级别的准确性，训练期间零工具调用，每个训练步骤比OPD快至少4倍。

Conclusion: ReOPD将昂贵的智能体-环境交互转化为可重用的离线资源，实现了跨工具、任务和环境的可扩展蒸馏。

Abstract: We study on-policy distillation (OPD) for agentic tasks, where an LLM agent interacts with an environment over multiple turns and a student imitates a teacher over these multi-turn interaction histories. Fully online OPD is costly because each update requires fresh student rollouts through the environment and teacher queries at visited histories. We propose Replayed-Prefix On-Policy Distillation (ReOPD), an off-environment alternative that reuses pre-collected teacher trajectories as replayed prefixes: the student acts at selected steps, while the teacher provides dense per-step supervision without executing new environment interactions. We show that multi-turn OPD introduces a prefix trap: making histories more student-on-policy improves relevance to the student, but can query the teacher on histories where its target is unreliable. This creates a two-sided distribution shift between student occupancy and teacher reliability. ReOPD addresses this by treating multi-turn OPD as a reliability-aware prefix distribution design and implements it with a simple step-decaying sampling schedule that emphasizes early, lower-shift prefixes. Across mathematical reasoning with Python and search environments over multiple teacher and student model scales, ReOPD preserves or improves OPD-level accuracy, uses zero tool calls during student training, and is at least 4$\times$ faster per training step than OPD. ReOPD therefore turns expensive agent-environment interaction into a reusable offline resource, enabling scalable distillation across tools, tasks, and environments.

</details>


### [235] [MDL Meets Latent Confounders: LNML-based Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04133)
*Zhongyi Que,Shin Matsushima,Kenji Yamanishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于MDL的因果发现框架PCG-CD，通过最小化LNML码长来处理非线性机制和潜在混淆变量，并引入Δ-伪共线性概念识别混淆变量导致的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法在处理非线性机制和潜在混淆变量时存在局限，要么依赖线性假设，要么要求因果充分性（即无未观测混淆变量），限制了实际应用场景。

Method: 基于MDL（最小描述长度）原理，通过最小化幸运归一化最大似然（LNML）码长来建模因果结构；引入Δ-伪共线性概念识别由潜在混淆变量诱发的依赖关系；在此基础上设计贪婪算法PCG-CD进行因果发现。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，所提方法能准确恢复有向因果关系，并有效检测潜在混淆变量。

Conclusion: PCG-CD框架能够同时处理非线性机制和潜在混淆变量，突破了现有方法在线性假设和因果充分性方面的限制，具有更广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Causal discovery with nonlinear mechanisms and latent confounders remains challenging. Existing methods often rely on either linear assumptions or causal sufficiency, limiting their applicability. We propose an MDL-based causal discovery framework that explicitly accounts for latent confounders while allowing flexible nonlinear mechanisms by minimizing the luckiness normalized maximum likelihood (LNML) code-length. The causal relationship between each variable pair is determined by selecting the shortest code-length of the causal model, and we introduce the notion of $Δ$-pseudo-collinearity to identify dependencies induced by latent confounders. Based on these ideas, we develop a greedy algorithm, termed Pseudo-Collinearity Guided Causal Discovery (PCG-CD). Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method accurately recovers directed causal relationships and effectively detects latent confounders.

</details>


### [236] [Train Smarter, Not Longer: Memorization-Guided Data Reuse for Efficient LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04969)
*Jingwei Zuo,Cong Zeng,Ilyas Chahed,Maksim Velikanov,Dhia Eddine Rhaiem,Pasquale Balsebre,Abhay Kumar,Younes Belkada,Hakim Hacid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于"记忆窗口"信号的自适应数据重用训练范式（Memorization-guided Data Reuse），通过损失保留动态和下流评估分数来判定何时及如何重用数据，初步实验表明模型性能在远超传统四轮限制的重复训练中仍可持续提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练已从单次训练转向多轮训练，但过度重复会导致过拟合和收益递减。如何确定何时以及如何有效重用数据是一个尚未充分探索的关键问题。

Method: 通过观察模型的"记忆窗口"信号（基于损失保留动态和下流评估分数），提出一种自适应的数据重用训练范式，动态决定训练轮次和数据重放调度。

Result: 初步实验发现一致的记忆驱动机制：模型性能在远超当前实践（如常见的四轮限制）的重复训练中仍持续提升。

Conclusion: 这些发现为记忆感知的训练调度奠定了基础，有助于确定数据重用预算，推动在有限高质量数据下"更智能而非更长时间"地训练大语言模型。

Abstract: The training paradigm of large language models has shifted from traditional one-pass training to multi-epoch training, as reasonable reuse of limited high-quality data can improve both model performance and sample efficiency. Meanwhile, excessive repetition introduces the risk of overfitting and diminishing returns. Determining when and how to reuse data effectively thus emerges as a natural but under-explored question. Through a novel observation of model's "Memorization Window" signals derived from loss retention dynamics and downstream evaluation scores, we propose "Memorization-guided Data Reuse", a training paradigm that adaptively determines when and how data should be reused, enabling principled decisions on the number of training epochs and the scheduling of data replays. Our preliminary experiments reveal a consistent memorization-driven regime: performance continues to improve with repetition far beyond current practice (e.g., the commonly cited four-epoch limit). While a full scheduler remains future work, these insights provide a foundation for memorization-aware training schedules, helping to determine reuse budgets and move toward training LLMs smarter rather than longer with limited high-quality data.

</details>


### [237] [Masked Generative-Contrastive Representation Learning for Cross-Dataset EEG-Based Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04139)
*Huqin Weng,Jiayang Huang,Yimin Wen,Jie Du,Chi-Man Vong,Chuangquan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MGCRL，一种面向EEG情绪识别的掩码生成-对比表示学习框架，通过区域感知时空编码器、JEPA生成式学习与对比学习策略，在跨数据集上实现了细粒度与全局判别性表示，在FACED预训练和SEED系列数据集微调中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法难以捕捉EEG信号在不同通道配置下的复杂时空依赖、提取抗噪的细粒度表示以及学习跨主体泛化的全局特征，导致SSL在EEG情绪识别中的潜力尚未充分挖掘。

Method: 提出MGCRL框架，包含三个关键设计：1) 基于区域图卷积的时空编码器，捕获局部空间与功能关系，适应不同通道配置；2) 基于JEPA的生成式学习，利用掩码特征学习抗噪细粒度表示；3) 对比学习策略，利用掩码与原始特征学习时间稳定、跨主体不变的表示。

Result: 在FACED大型数据集上预训练，在多个SEED系列数据集上微调，实验验证了MGCRL的有效性。

Conclusion: MGCRL通过融合生成式与对比式自监督学习，有效解决了EEG情绪识别中通道配置差异、噪声干扰和跨主体泛化等关键挑战，展现出学习通用表示的能力。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) shows strong potential for cross-dataset transfer by improving feature representation and generalization. However, its application to EEG-based emotion recognition remains largely unexplored. Existing SSL methods struggle to capture the intricate spatiotemporal dependencies of EEG signals under varying channel configurations, extract fine-grained representations resilient to noise, and derive global features that generalize well across subjects. To address these challenges, we propose Masked Generative-Contrastive Representation Learning (MGCRL), a novel SSL framework specifically designed for EEG-based emotion recognition. Built upon a region-aware spatiotemporal encoder, MGCRL integrates generative and contrastive learning to achieve both fine-grained and global discriminative representations for cross-dataset generalization. MGCRL introduces three key designs: 1) a spatiotemporal encoder that incorporates region-based graph convolution to capture localized spatial and functional relationships, enhancing region-specific feature learning and mitigating the impact of varying EEG channel configurations across datasets; 2) a generative learning mechanism based on the joint embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) that utilizes masked features to capture noise robustness fine-grained representations, improving the model's capability to characterize subtle emotional states; and 3) a contrastive learning strategy that leverages masked and original features to learn temporally stable and cross-subject-invariant representations across the same stimuli, boosting emotion discrimination and cross-subject generalization. Under these designs, MGCRL exhibits remarkable ability to learn universal representation. Extensive experiments involving pretraining on the large FACED dataset and fine-tuning on multiple SEED-series datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of MGCRL.

</details>


### [238] [Localized LoRA-MoE: Block-wise Low-Rank Experts With Adaptive Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05114)
*Babak Barazandeh,Subhabrata Majumdar,Vinay Prithyani,George Michailidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Localized LoRA-MoE框架，通过局部化空间阻塞与动态上下文路由解决LoRA在动态任务切换和传感器故障中的梯度冲突问题。评估了集中式宏路由（Block-Wise）和去中心化微路由（Cell-Wise）两种范式，实验表明去中心化门控能实现统计最优，起到"梯度防火墙"作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LoRA因单瓶颈结构在多任务流中易发生梯度冲突，导致适配器权重坍缩为非专业化均值；现有空间分区方法受限于静态拓扑，无法适应动态任务切换或传感器故障。

Method: 提出Localized LoRA-MoE框架，包含两种架构：Block-Wise LoRA-MoE（集中式宏路由，通过整体上下文信号调控整个结构网格）和Cell-Wise LoRA-MoE（去中心化微路由，每个坐标单元拥有自主的局部专家门控）。

Result: 两种架构均解决了静态基线的优化死锁；去中心化单元级门控达到了与全知全局协调器统计等价的效果，提供了"梯度防火墙"保护未受干扰路径免受故障传播影响，且持续优于静态基线。

Conclusion: 所提方法为跨细粒度坐标场和动态运行场景下的模型自适应提供了可扩展、参数高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and high-dimensional perception networks increasingly rely on parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) to adapt to diverse operational contexts. However, standard methods like LoRA are structurally limited by a monolithic bottleneck, making them highly susceptible to gradient warfare. Interleaved multi-task streams may trigger destructive optimization feedback, collapsing adapter weights into unspecialized averages. While recent spatial partitioning methods have introduced block-wise isolation, they remain trapped in static topologies, unable to adapt to dynamic task-switching or environmental sensor failure. In this work, we introduce Localized LoRA-MoE, a unified framework that fuses localized spatial blocking with dynamic, context-conditioned routing. We propose and evaluate two novel architectural paradigms: Block-Wise LoRA-MoE (Centralized Macro-Routing), which modulates the entire structural grid via a monolithic context signal, and Cell-Wise LoRA-MoE (Decentralized Micro-Routing), which empowers every coordinate cell in the matrix grid with autonomous, localized expert gating. Through a comprehensive suite of benchmarks, ranging from high-dimensional SVD matrix simulations and real-world tabular transformations to spatial vision perception under sensor degradation, we demonstrate that both architectures resolve optimization deadlocks inherent in static baselines. Our empirical results establish that decentralized cell-level gating achieves complete statistical parity with an omniscient global coordinator, providing a robust "gradient firewall" that protects surviving pathways from fault-propagated corruption. Our proposals consistently outperform static baselines, offering a scalable and parameter-efficient solution for dynamic model adaptation across granular coordinate fields and shifting operational regimes.

</details>


### [239] [Binary Iterative Method for Non-targeted Adversarial Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04145)
*Naman Goyal,Milan Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“二元迭代法”（BinIM）的新型非目标对抗攻击方法，采用分治策略优化超参数，在黑盒分类任务中（InceptionV3/V2、ResNet V2 152）优于FGSM、BIM、VAM等现有梯度方法，在ImageNet上将分类器对真实标签的置信度降至接近零（2.21e-09），同时使错误分类置信度高达0.995。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非目标对抗攻击方法（如快速梯度法、基础迭代法、虚拟对抗法）在寻找最优输入数据点和超参数方面仍存在挑战，需要更高效的方法来生成高质量的非目标对抗样本。

Method: 提出Binary Iterative Method (BinIM)，采用分治（divide-and-conquer）范式来优化参数和超参数，用于生成非目标对抗攻击。

Result: 在ImageNet的1000张随机采样图像上，BinIM在所有梯度方法中表现最优，使分类器以高达0.995的置信度错误分类，同时将真实标签的概率降至约2.21e-09。

Conclusion: BinIM作为一种新的非目标对抗攻击方法，通过分治策略有效优化了攻击参数，显著优于现有梯度方法，适用于鲁棒性测试。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks guide and provide additional training and test data for both adversarial training and adversarial robustness validation, and expose the 'piecewise linearity' of deep learning based models. Since adversarial attacks and adversarial robustness are mathematically defined problems that can be optimised directly with end-to-end differentiable search, adversarial robustness is more widely applicable than other robustness metrics such as corruption and perturbation robustness, and new kinds of adversarial attacks are beneficial for robustness testing. Attacks are targeted or non-targeted depending on whether the image is modified to misclassify to a particular class or to any incorrect class; we focus on the non-targeted setting. Finding the optimal input data points and hyper-parameters for generating non-targeted adversarial attacks remains a challenge for current methods like the Fast Gradient Method, Basic Iterative Method and Virtual Adversarial Method. We propose a new method, the "Binary Iterative Method" (BinIM), which uses a divide-and-conquer paradigm to optimise parameters and hyper-parameters for the generation of non-targeted attacks. We compare our method to other gradient-based adversarial attacks evaluated over pre-trained networks (InceptionV3, InceptionV2, ResNet V2 152) on classification tasks. On 1000 randomly-sampled images from the standard ImageNet dataset, the Binary Iterative Method outperforms all other gradient-based methods, qualitatively making the classifier misclassify with confidence up to 0.995 while reducing the probability of the true label to 2.21e-09 (approximately 0).

</details>


### [240] [Mask-based Predictive Representations for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04153)
*Kai Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于掩码预测的自监督辅助任务，结合Transformer在潜在空间重建被掩码的输入序列，以从高维图像输入中学习有效表征，提升强化学习的样本效率，在多个连续和离散控制基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于视觉的深度强化学习中，从高维图像输入和有限样本中提取有效状态表示以实现样本高效强化学习的问题。

Method: 提出基于掩码预测的自监督辅助任务，利用智能体从环境中收集的序列信息及序列中的上下文信息预测被掩码的信息，在潜在空间中通过Transformer重建被掩码的输入序列，无需重建原始像素（非重构方法）。

Result: 该方法学习的压缩表示输入到强化学习模型后，提升了强化学习的样本效率，在多个连续和离散控制基准上优于最先进的样本高效强化学习方法。

Conclusion: 基于掩码预测的非重构自监督辅助任务结合Transformer，能够有效从高维图像中学习有效表征，提升强化学习样本效率。

Abstract: Vision-based deep reinforcement learning involves dealing with high-dimensional inputs of image information. It is crucial to abstract effective states from high-dimensional image inputs and limited samples for sample-efficient reinforcement learning. To address this challenge, inspired by fields such as natural language processing and computer vision, we propose a self-supervised task based on mask prediction as an auxiliary task for reinforcement learning. This non-reconstruction method uses the sequence information collected by the agent from the environment and the context information in the sequence to predict the masked information, thereby strengthening the agent's understanding of the task and learning effective representations. Combined with transformers, we find that the model reconstructs the masked input sequence in the latent space. By feeding the compressed representations learned by this method into reinforcement learning models, we observe an improvement in the sample efficiency of reinforcement learning. Moreover, the model outperforms state-of-the-art sample-efficient reinforcement learning methods on multiple continuous and discrete control benchmarks.

</details>


### [241] [Noisy-Channel Minimum Bayes Risk Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05198)
*Yusuke Sakai,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于噪声信道分解的MBR解码方法，通过引入双向效应对抗不对称评估指标，将MBR分解为四个交互组件（假设→参考似然、参考→假设似然、假设先验、参考先验），统一了现有MBR变体，并揭示了不同指标下通道贡献的差异性与跨任务一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MBR解码在假设选择时仅计算假设到参考的单向期望效用，但常用评估指标（如BLEU、COMET）本身是不对称的，这种设计上的不匹配可能导致次优选择。作者旨在通过引入双向通道效应来弥补这一差距。

Method: 将MBR解码进行噪声信道分解，将其拆分为四个交互组件：假设到参考似然（hypothesis-to-reference likelihood）、参考到假设似然（reference-to-hypothesis likelihood）、假设先验（hypothesis prior）和参考先验（reference prior）。这种分解统一了现有MBR变体，并允许对每个通道的贡献进行隔离分析。

Result: 实验表明，不同评估指标下各通道的贡献呈现显著差异，但跨任务保持一致性。适当的通道加权可能进一步提升原始MBR解码的性能。

Conclusion: 噪声信道分解为MBR解码提供了统一的理论框架，揭示了双向不对称性的重要性，并为不同指标和任务提供了可解释性，为后续优化通道权重以提升生成质量指明了方向。

Abstract: Minimum Bayes Risk (MBR) decoding yields more robust and higher-quality text generation than maximum a posteriori (MAP) decoding by selecting hypotheses that maximize expected utility over sampled pseudo-references. However, there exists a discrepancy in the design: hypothesis selection calculates expected utility scores conditioned on given pseudo-references, while commonly used evaluation metrics, e.g., BLEU and COMET, are asymmetric. Therefore, it is important to consider both hypothesis-to-reference and reference-to-hypothesis directional effects. In this study, we introduce a noisy channel decomposition of MBR decoding that naturally incorporates bidirectional effects to account for these asymmetries. We decompose MBR decoding into four interacting components: hypothesis-to-reference likelihood, reference-to-hypothesis likelihood, hypothesis prior, and reference prior. This decomposition provides a unified interpretation of existing MBR variants and enables metric- and task-specific interpretability by isolating the contribution of each channel. Our comprehensive analysis reveals that channel-wise contributions exhibit distinct characteristics across metrics while remaining consistent across tasks, and suggests that appropriate channel weighting may lead to improvements over original MBR decoding.

</details>


### [242] [Geometry of Ordinal Representations in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04167)
*Saksham Bassi,Sharvi Tomar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型在四个序数任务（括号深度、缩进、表格位置、数值大小）中的表示几何，发现当序数变量可从token身份局部计算时，会涌现出带有位置细胞特征平铺的1D流形；而需要跨位置整合或语义提取的任务则产生高维或非相干表示。几何计算依赖于架构，激活修补证实了流形子空间集中了任务相关信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在序数任务上的表示几何是否具有普适性，验证此前关于字符计数的1D流形和注意力头几何变换的发现能否推广到更多任务和模型。

Method: 在Gemma-2-2B、Gemma-2-9B和Qwen3-4B三个模型上，对四个序数任务（括号深度、缩进、表格位置、数值大小）进行表示分析，包括流形维度测量、几何变换检测、激活修补和流形方向消融实验。

Result: 1) 局部可计算的序数任务涌现1D流形和位置细胞特征平铺；2) 需要跨位置整合或语义提取的任务产生高维或非相干表示；3) 几何计算依赖架构，Qwen3-4B在缩进任务中表现出比Gemma模型更强的扭转，且其扭转器能保持序数顺序；4) 流形方向消融导致探针准确率大幅下降，证实了流形子空间的任务相关性。

Conclusion: 语言模型中的序数表示几何具有条件普适性，其形式取决于任务的计算需求和模型架构特性。流形子空间确实是任务相关信息的关键载体。

Abstract: Recent work showed that language models represent character counts on curved 1D manifolds, with attention heads performing geometric transformations to enable computation. We test whether this generalizes across four ordinal tasks (bracket depth, indentation, table position, numeric magnitude) in Gemma-2-2B, Gemma-2-9B, and Qwen3-4B. We find that 1D manifolds with place-cell feature tiling emerge for tasks where the ordinal variable is locally computable from token identity, while tasks requiring cross-position integration or semantic extraction produce higher-dimensional or incoherent representations. Geometric computation is architecture-dependent: Qwen3-4B shows substantially stronger twisting than Gemma models for indentation, and its twisters preserve ordinal order, unlike its numeric twisters. Activation patching confirms that the identified manifold subspaces concentrate task-relevant information, with manifold-direction ablation causing dramatically larger probe accuracy drops than random-direction controls.

</details>


### [243] [What Does a Discrete Diffusion Model Learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05381)
*Rodrigo Casado Noguerales,Bernhard Schölkopf,Thomas Hofmann,Aran Raoufi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格推导了连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）的证据下界（ELBO），证明负ELBO精确等于数据熵加上学习反向过程与Oracle反向过程的路径KL散度，其唯一优化器是给定当前噪声状态下真实反向跳变速率的条件期望。对于具有token因子化噪声的序列，优化器有三种等价坐标形式：去噪器、cavity（桥接插件）和分数，并提供了它们之间的闭式转换。该框架统一了MDM、UDM、SEDD、GIDD等现有方法，解释了掩码扩散与均匀扩散的区别，并验证了ELBO实现在初始化时的精确校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型中神经网络在不同坐标（去噪器、分数比、桥接插件预测器）下表示相同的对象，但使用错误坐标会导致训练和采样过程不匹配。现有文献缺乏对这些坐标关系的严格理论理解，需要一个统一框架来澄清每种损失函数实际优化的目标。

Method: 从CTMC ELBO的严格推导出发，证明Oracle Distance定理（负ELBO精确等于数据熵加路径KL散度），识别优化器为条件期望；针对token因子化噪声，推导了三种坐标（去噪器、cavity、分数）的闭式转换关系；在可精确求解模型上进行了数值验证。

Result: 1) 证明了负ELBO的精确分解（非仅上界）；2) 揭示了任意噪声过程共享相同的最优负ELBO值——数据熵；3) 统一了MDM、UDM、SEDD、GIDD等模型；4) 解释了去噪器与cavity在掩码扩散中相等但在均匀扩散中不等的原因；5) 证明了均匀扩散中去噪器参数化导致ELBO在初始化时发散，而桥接插件参数化保持有限。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型的学习目标在三种坐标下等价，但不同参数化对训练数值稳定性和采样行为有显著影响。该框架为理解和设计离散扩散模型提供了理论基础，并给出了ELBO实现的精确初始化校准方法。

Abstract: What does a discrete diffusion model learn: a denoiser, a score ratio, or a bridge plug-in predictor? At the level of jump rates, these are one object in different coordinates, and reading a neural network in the wrong coordinate changes the process being trained and sampled. Starting with a rigorous derivation of the continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) ELBO for any noising process, boundary terms included, we prove the \emph{Oracle Distance} theorem: the negative ELBO is exactly equal to the data entropy plus the path KL from the oracle reverse process to the learned one, not merely a bound. Its unique optimizer is therefore the conditional expectation of the true reverse jump rate given the current noisy state, and its irreducible cost is the rate at which the forward process $Z_t$ destroys information about the clean data $Z_0$, $-\tfrac{d}{dt}I(Z_0; Z_t)$, so every noising process shares the same best achievable negative ELBO: the data entropy. For sequences with token-factorizing noise, the oracle projection yields three exact coordinates for the optimizer: denoiser, cavity (bridge plug-in), and score, with closed-form conversions among them. This framework identifies which law each loss in the literature actually optimizes, recovering MDM, UDM, SEDD, and GIDD as special cases; explains why denoiser and cavity coincide for masked diffusion but not for uniform diffusion; proves that a denoiser parameterization makes the uniform ELBO diverge at initialization while the bridge plug-in stays finite; and calibrates ELBO implementations exactly at initialization. Every identity is verified numerically, without approximation, on an exactly solvable model.

</details>


### [244] [FedFFT: Taming Client Drift in Federated SAM via Spectral Perturbation Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04170)
*Liyang Yuan,Yibo Yang,Dandan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedFFT方法，通过频域滤波去除SAM扰动中的低频不一致成分，以解决联邦学习中客户端漂移和泛化差的问题，在非独立同分布场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中统计异质性导致客户端漂移和锐利极小值，SAM虽能改善泛化但存在扰动不一致导致的发散问题，现有方法缺乏对扰动不一致性本质的理解。

Method: 从频域角度分析发现客户端间SAM扰动的不一致性主要集中于低频分量，据此提出FedFFT，通过滤波去除低频扰动分量，是一种轻量级即插即用方法，无需额外通信。

Result: 在多个基准和骨干网络上的实验表明，FedFFT一致优于现有的SAM联邦学习方法，尤其在严重非独立同分布数据分布下优势明显。

Conclusion: 频域视角为锐度感知联邦优化提供了有效、可扩展且通用性强的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables decentralized training without data sharing, but suffers from statistical heterogeneity across clients, leading to client drift, poor generalization, and sharp minima compared to centralized training. Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) has emerged as a promising approach to improve generalization, yet its application in federated learning still suffers from divergence problems, since perturbations are computed locally and reflect client-specific loss geometries. To better understand this issue, we provide experimental evidence from a new perspective, the frequency domain, for SAM perturbations in federated settings, revealing that inter-client perturbation inconsistencies are predominantly concentrated in the low-frequency spectrum. Motivated by this insight, we propose Federated learning with Frequency-domain Filtering of SAM perturbations (FedFFT). It is a lightweight and plug-and-play method that filters out low-frequency components of SAM perturbations without requiring additional communication, thereby suppressing inconsistent components in client updates while preserving consistent learning signals. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks and diverse backbones demonstrate that FedFFT consistently outperforms SAM-based FL methods, particularly under severe non-IID distributions. These results highlight the effectiveness, scalability, and general applicability of our frequency-domain perspective for sharpness-aware federated optimization.

</details>


### [245] [Physics-Informed Graph Learning with Uncertainty Awareness for Open-Set Domain Generalization in Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04188)
*Jinfeng Zhu,Shiyu Long,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PGU-OD框架，一种物理信息引导的图学习与不确定性感知方法，用于开放集域泛化下的旋转机械故障诊断。通过物理信息谱注意力模块、不确定性感知自适应图学习机制和高斯分布自适应边界损失函数，有效抑制了频率偏移导致的感知不确定性、结构传播不确定性，并可靠拒识未知故障。在公开数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能工业维护中旋转机械的可靠故障诊断面临两大挑战：未知故障类型（开放集）和工况变化导致的域偏移（域泛化）。现有方法主要基于数据驱动，忽略了特征提取、拓扑学习和决策阶段级联传播的不确定性。

Method: （1）物理信息谱注意力模块：提取工况鲁棒的故障特征，抑制频率偏移引起的感知不确定性。（2）不确定性感知自适应图学习机制：利用类别尺度的高斯分布参数动态调整样本图的边权重，缓解结构不确定性传播。（3）高斯分布自适应边界损失函数与双准则开放集推理策略：优化决策边界并可靠拒识未知故障。

Result: 在两个公开旋转机械故障数据集上的实验表明，PGU-OD在域偏移条件下，无论是已知故障分类还是未知故障拒识，均优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: PGU-OD通过物理信息引导与不确定性感知的有机结合，有效解决了开放集域泛化下的级联不确定性传播问题，为智能工业维护中的故障诊断提供了一种新的有效范式。

Abstract: Intelligent industrial maintenance critically relies on reliable fault diagnosis of rotating machinery. However, it faces formidable challenges from unknown fault types and domain shifts induced by varying operating conditions, which is formally formulated as the open-set domain generalization (OSDG) problem. Existing methods are mainly data-driven, thereby overlooking the cascaded propagation of uncertainty across feature extraction, topological learning, and decision-making stages.To tackle this challenge, we propose PGU-OD, a novel Physics-Informed Graph Learning framework with Uncertainty Awareness for Open-set Domain generalization. First, it designs a physics-informed spectral attention module to extract condition-robust fault features, thereby suppressing perceptual uncertainty caused by frequency shifts. Further, it constructs an uncertainty aware adaptive graph learning mechanism to dynamically adjust the edge weights of the sample graph guided by class-scale Gaussian distribution parameters, which mitigates the structural propagation of uncertainty. Finally, a Gaussian-distribution-based adaptive boundary loss function and a dual-criteria open-set inference strategy are developed to optimize decision boundaries and reliably reject unknown faults. Extensive experimental evaluations on two public and widely used rotating machinery fault datasets demonstrate that the proposed PGU-OD outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both known fault classification and unknown fault rejection under domain shifts.

</details>


### [246] [Weak-to-Strong Generalization via Direct On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05394)
*Shiyuan Feng,Huan-ang Gao,Haohan Chi,Hanlin Wu,Zhilong Zhang,Zheng Jiang,Bingxiang He,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Direct-OPD方法，通过将弱模型RL训练前后的策略偏移作为隐式奖励信号，蒸馏到强模型上，避免在大模型上重复昂贵的RL训练，显著提升强模型推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR虽能提升语言模型推理能力，但在每个新强模型上重复RL训练代价高昂，因为目标模型需在训练中生成大量rollout。随着模型规模扩大，后训练本身成为瓶颈。因此希望将小模型上的RL收益迁移到大模型，避免重复训练。

Method: 提出Direct On-Policy Distillation (Direct-OPD)。将弱教师模型RL后的checkpoint与其RL前的reference checkpoints对比，计算log-ratio作为稠密隐式奖励信号；在强学生的on-policy状态上施加该信号，直接复用弱模型的RL监督信号，无需训练显式奖励模型或在目标模型上运行稀疏奖励RL。

Result: Direct-OPD能持续利用弱教师提升强目标模型；在AIME 2024上，Qwen3-1.7B从48.3%提升至62.4%，仅需8张A100 GPU训练4小时；优于步骤匹配的直接RL，并支持多个策略偏移的顺序组合。

Conclusion: RL训练的成果可以作为隐式奖励信号跨模型规模复用，而不只是作为最终模型进行模仿。Direct-OPD通过策略偏移的蒸馏实现了高效、可扩展的弱到强迁移。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a powerful recipe for improving language-model reasoning, but it is expensive to repeat on every new strong model because the target model must generate many rollouts during training. As models scale, post-training itself becomes a bottleneck. We study a weak-to-strong alternative: run RL on a smaller model where rollouts are cheaper, then reuse what that RL run learned to improve a stronger target model. Directly distilling the post-RL weak teacher is not enough, because the teacher's final policy mixes useful RL gains with the limitations of the smaller model. We propose Direct On-Policy Distillation (Direct-OPD), which transfers the teacher's RL-induced policy shift instead. Direct-OPD compares the post-RL teacher with its own pre-RL reference and treats their log-ratio as a dense implicit reward for the student. In plain terms, the checkpoint pair tells us which actions RL made the weak model more or less likely to take, and Direct-OPD applies that signal on the stronger student's own on-policy states. This directly reuses the weak model's RL supervision signal without training an explicit reward model or running sparse-reward RL on the target model. Empirically, Direct-OPD consistently leverages weaker teachers to improve stronger target models; notably, it boosts Qwen3-1.7B from 48.3% to 62.4% on AIME 2024 in just 4 hours on 8 A100 GPUs. It outperforms step-matched direct RL and enables the sequential composition of multiple policy shifts. Our results show that RL outcomes can be reused across model scales as implicit reward signals, not merely as final models to imitate.

</details>


### [247] [SpecGradFilter: A Spectral Gradient Filtering Framework for Taming Federated Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04189)
*Liyang Yuan,Yibo Yang,Dandan Guo,Peter Richtarik,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从频域角度重新审视联邦学习中的客户端漂移问题，发现漂移主要集中在低频分量上，据此提出统一的光谱梯度过滤框架SpecGradFilter，通过抑制不一致的低频信号来缓解统计异质性，在高度Non-IID场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法主要从空间域进行梯度校正或正则化来缓解统计异质性导致的客户端漂移，但忽略了优化信号的固有频谱结构。

Method: 提出SpecGradFilter光谱梯度过滤框架，通过频域分析发现客户端梯度差异主要集中在低频分量，从而通过抑制低频信号（如FFT截断或高斯去趋势等空间近似）来缓解漂移。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100和Tiny-ImageNet等基准测试中，SpecGradFilter在高度Non-IID设置下显著提升了性能，且通信开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: SpecGradFilter是一种可泛化的原则性方法，建立了鲁棒联邦优化的新范式。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is fundamentally challenged by statistical heterogeneity, where non-identically distributed (non-IID) data induces client drift that severely hampers global convergence. While existing approaches attempt to mitigate this drift through spatial-domain gradient correction or regularization, they overlook the intrinsic spectral structure of optimization signals. In this work, we revisit client drift from a novel frequency-domain perspective and uncover a critical Spectral Bias of Drift: inter-client gradient divergence is predominantly concentrated in low-frequency components which encode client-specific distributional shifts, while high-frequency components representing fine-grained features remain relatively consistent. Motivated by this, we propose SpecGradFilter, a unified Spectral Gradient Filtering Framework that tames heterogeneity by suppressing discordant low-frequency signals. Crucially, we demonstrate that SpecGradFilter is a generalizable principle, effective not only via precise FFT-based truncation but also through spatial approximations like Gaussian detrending. Extensive experiments on benchmarks such as CIFAR-10/100 and Tiny-ImageNet demonstrate that SpecGradFilter significantly performs better performance in highly Non-IID settings with negligible communication overhead, establishing a new paradigm for robust federated optimization.

</details>


### [248] [Exploring Convolutional Neural Processes for Weather Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04190)
*Francisco Passos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于卷积条件神经过程(ConvCNP)对瑞士复杂地形区域的ERA5-Land日最高气温进行统计降尺度，从~11km分辨率提升至~1km。最优模型MAE为1.31°C，CRPS技能分0.524，相比双线性插值降低了超过一半的预期预测误差。消融研究表明高程MLP不可或缺，但模型存在过度自信的不确定性估计问题，且零样本迁移至离网格观测站时无法获得正技能分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球再分析产品(如ERA5-Land)在山区等复杂地形区域分辨率过粗(约11km)，无法捕捉数公里范围内温度变化达数度的局地细节，需要统计降尺度方法来提升空间分辨率以满足局地应用需求。

Method: 采用卷积条件神经过程(ConvCNP)架构，基于Vaughan et al.(2022)工作进行改进，融入瑞士高分辨率高程特征(DHM25)，使用2014-2023年十年数据进行五折时序交叉验证训练，并开展消融研究评估各组件贡献。

Result: 最优模型MAE为1.31°C，CRPS技能分0.524；高程MLP是不可或缺的组件，缺失时模型完全发散；季节特征和地形位置指数提供次要增益；稀疏网格输入下模型在保留约10%输入网格时仍保持正技能；零样本离网格站点部署在所有测试密度下均无法获得正技能；所有配置均表现出严重过自信的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: ConvCNP是复杂地形气候降尺度的可行有效方法，但不确定度校准和对非网格输入的原生支持是实际部署的关键挑战。

Abstract: Global reanalysis products such as ERA5-Land provide spatially complete weather fields but at resolutions too coarse for local applications, particularly in mountainous regions where temperature can vary by several degrees over short distances. This project investigates Convolutional Conditional Neural Processes (ConvCNPs) for statistical downscaling of daily maximum temperature from the ~11km resolution ERA5-Land grid to ~1km resolution over Switzerland, building upon the architecture of Vaughan et al. (2022) and adapting it to the topographically complex Swiss domain with high-resolution elevation features from the swisstopo DHM25. The best model, trained on ten years of data (2014-2023) with five-fold temporal cross-validation, achieves a mean absolute error of 1.31 Celsius and a CRPS-based skill score of 0.524 relative to bilinear interpolation, reducing the expected prediction error by more than half. An ablation study reveals that the elevation MLP is the indispensable component - without it, the model diverges entirely - while explicit seasonal features and Topographic Position Index provide secondary benefits. Under sparse on-grid input the model degrades gracefully, maintaining positive skill down to approximately 10% of the input grid; however, zero-shot deployment on off-grid station observations does not achieve positive skill at any density tested. All configurations exhibit severely overconfident uncertainty estimates, a structural limitation of the Gaussian likelihood training objective. These results demonstrate that ConvCNPs are a viable and effective approach to climate downscaling in complex terrain, and identify uncertainty calibration and native support for non-gridded input as the key challenges for operational deployment.

</details>


### [249] [Channel-Adaptive Robust Aggregation for Over-the-Air Federated Learning in Heterogeneous Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04218)
*Zubaida Fatima,Zubair Shaban,Yusuf Jamal,Nazreen Shah,Ranjitha Prasad,B. N. Bharath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CHARGE-FL框架，通过信道自适应调度聚合和双用途预编码机制，在6G网络的Over-the-Air联邦学习中联合解决噪声、衰落和客户端异构性问题，显著提升准确率、稳定性和收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Over-the-Air联邦学习方法依赖固定的聚合调度，未能联合解决噪声、信道衰落和客户端异质性（如掉队者）问题，限制了在6G隐私敏感型应用中的性能。

Method: 提出CHARGE-FL框架，包含：(1) 基于信道动态和应用就绪度的自适应聚合调度策略；(2) 定制化优化策略；(3) 双用途预编码机制，同时缓解信道失真和部分更新带来的偏差。

Result: 在真实无线条件下，相比现有OTA-FL方法，CHARGE-FL在准确率、稳定性和收敛性方面显著提升，尤其在存在掉队者和高噪声场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: CHARGE-FL通过信道自适应的鲁棒聚合机制，有效克服了OTA-FL在无线环境中的关键挑战，为6G网络中隐私保护的数据密集型应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The growing demand for privacy-preserving, data-intensive applications such as IoT, augmented reality, and autonomous systems positions Federated Learning (FL) as a key enabler in 6G networks. Over-the-Air FL (OTA-FL) leverages the superposition property of the wireless multiple access channel for efficient aggregation via simultaneous transmissions. Existing methods rely on fixed aggregation schedules and do not jointly address noise, fading, and client heterogeneity. We propose CHARGE-FL (CHannel-Adaptive Robust agGrEgation), a framework that adaptively schedules aggregation based on channel dynamics and application readiness. By combining a tailored optimization strategy with a dual-purpose precoding mechanism, CHARGE-FL mitigates channel distortion and bias from partial updates, achieving superior accuracy, stability, and convergence under realistic wireless conditions. Empirical results under realistic wireless conditions show that CHARGE-FL significantly improves accuracy, stability, and convergence over state-of-the-art OTA-FL methods, particularly in straggler-prone and noisy scenarios.

</details>


### [250] [Quantize the Target, Quantize the Drafter: Efficient Inference with Qwen3.5-4B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04244)
*Jaeyeon Kim,Jewon Lee,Bo-Kyeong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合量化感知蒸馏与块扩散草稿模型的推测解码系统，在A10G GPU上实现Qwen3.5-4B的低延迟推理，取得6.978倍加速并排名第三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的NVIDIA A10G GPU上实现Qwen3.5-4B的低延迟服务，目标是克服量化带来的精度损失并加速解码过程。

Method: 1) 对目标模型进行量化感知蒸馏，保留原始量化网格以恢复精度；2) 训练一个块扩散草稿模型，采用两阶段过程：先学习高精度目标，再适应低精度目标；3) 对草稿模型进一步量化并采用滑动窗口注意力，以减少开销并保持长上下文下的解码延迟。

Result: 在满足质量阈值的前提下，相比基线实现了6.978倍的平均加速，整体排名第三。

Conclusion: 该方法有效结合了量化与推测解码，为实际LLM推理中的低延迟部署提供了可行思路。

Abstract: This report describes our approach to the Efficient Qwen Competition, where the goal is to enable low-latency serving of Qwen3.5-4B on a resource-constrained NVIDIA A10G GPU. Our system combines a quantized target model with speculative decoding. To recover accuracy, we apply quantization-aware distillation to the target model while retaining the original quantization grid. To speed up decoding, a block-diffusion drafter specialized for the quantized target model is trained using a two-stage procedure: first learning from the high-precision target and then adapting to the low-precision target. Because the drafter is invoked at every speculative decoding step, we further reduce its overhead with quantization and sliding-window attention, preserving draft-token acceptance while improving long-context decoding latency. As a result, our submission achieves a 6.978$\times$ average speedup over the baseline while satisfying the required quality thresholds, ranking 3rd overall. We hope these results provide useful insights for practical LLM inference. The code and resources are available at https://github.com/nota-github/adaptfm-quant-dflash

</details>


### [251] [Signal or Noise? Understanding Generative Models for Real-World Sensor Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04245)
*Zitao Shuai,Zongzhe Xu,Yuntian Wu,Sirui Li,Tianhong Li,Yuzhe Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SensorGen是一项大规模传感器信号生成研究，覆盖14种设置、4个领域、7个数据集和12种信号模态，系统评估了五类生成模型，发现流匹配模型整体表现最优，信号属性对生成质量有显著影响，且生成信号在下游任务中具有实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传感器数据的生成建模在模态、数据集和任务公式上高度碎片化，缺乏对生成模型在真实世界场景中何时、如何以及为何成功或失败的系统性理解。

Method: 构建SensorGen框架，涵盖4个领域、7个数据集、12种信号模态和14种设置，对五大生成模型家族进行系统评估。

Result: (1) 流匹配模型在大多数设置中表现最佳；(2) 信号属性至关重要，人口统计协变量改善纵向生成，时频建模改善高频信号生成；(3) 生成信号具有实际效用，扩展提升生成质量，合成数据改善下游性能。

Conclusion: SensorGen建立了对真实世界传感器数据生成中设计选择、评估协议和失败模式的更广泛理解。

Abstract: Generative models have changed how machine learning represents complex data distributions, especially in language and vision, yet many real-world systems are observed instead as continuous, high-dimensional, and noisy sensor time series. Existing generative modeling of sensor data, however, remains fragmented across modalities, datasets, and task formulations, limiting a systematic understanding of when, how, and why generative models succeed or fail in real-world settings. To address this gap, we introduce SensorGen, a large-scale study of sensor-signal generation spanning 14 settings across 4 domains, 7 datasets, and 12 signal modalities. Leveraging SensorGen, we systematically evaluate generative models from five major families and uncover three key findings: (1) flow-matching models provide strong overall performance across most settings; (2) signal properties matter, with demographic covariates improving longitudinal generation and time-frequency modeling improving high-frequency signal generation; and (3) generated signals have practical utility beyond visual realism, with scaling improving generation quality and synthetic data improving downstream performance. Together, SensorGen establishes a broader understanding of design choices, evaluation protocols, and failure modes in real-world sensor data generation.

</details>


### [252] [On Preserving Geometrical Invariance for Superpixel Image Classification using Graph Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04262)
*Sarabeshwar Balaji,Shubham Mohanty,Akash Anil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SuperGT框架，利用图Transformer对超像素图进行图像分类，在保持平移/旋转不变性的同时捕获长距离依赖，在CIFAR-10上优于多数基线，性能与ShapeGNN相当但无需边界点坐标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNN和ViT处理密集像素网格存在计算冗余和复杂度高的问题，而现有基于超像素的GNN方法难以捕获长距离依赖且缺乏平移/旋转不变性，需要更高效的图像分类方案。

Method: 提出SuperGT框架：1) 将图像表示为超像素图，去除像素级冗余；2) 采用图Transformer架构捕获长距离依赖；3) 设计预处理方案保持平移/旋转不变性。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集上，SuperGT显著优于多个基线模型，性能与先前最先进的ShapeGNN相当，且不需要ShapeGNN所需的超像素边界点坐标信息。

Conclusion: 图Transformer结合超像素表示能有效平衡计算效率与分类性能，同时保持几何不变性，为图像分类提供了一种高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) and Vision Transformer (ViT) for image classification exploit a dense grid of pixels containing redundant information. Consequently, for a larger image dataset, CNNs and ViTs face deployability challenges due to high computational complexity. Representing images as graphs of superpixels offers an efficient alternative that preserves key information while eliminating pixel-level redundancy. Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have been utilized on such graphs to perform image classification. However, GNNs are known to struggle with capturing long-range dependencies which is important in the domain of image classification. Furthermore, a majority of these superpixel-based image classification approaches do not explicitly preserve translation/rotation invariance. Nevertheless, preserving translation/rotation invariance is important for robust image classification. Thus, this paper proposes SuperGT, a Graph Transformer-based framework for image classification, which captures the long range dependencies, along with a pre-processing scheme that preserves translation/rotation invariance. We evaluate SuperGT on CIFAR-10 dataset and observe that it performs significantly better than many baselines. Furthermore, we note that the overall performance of SuperGT is comparable to the previous state-of-the-art model, namely, ShapeGNN, without relying on coordinates of the boundary points of each superpixel required by ShapeGNN.

</details>


### [253] [SAD-LoRA: Spectral Alignment for Low-Rank Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04306)
*Omer Tariq,Syed Muhammad Raza,Jeongbae Son*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SAD-LoRA方法，通过谱对齐蒸馏来显式控制LoRA适配器的子空间，使数据加权蒸馏误差分解为子空间错位、系数不匹配和不可约秩残差三项，实验证明子空间对齐是提升压缩效率的关键组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的知识蒸馏将微调教师模型压缩为LoRA适配学生模型时，输出级蒸馏无法显式控制适配器占据的秩r权重子空间，导致子空间错位问题未被有效解决。

Method: 从数据加权的学生空间参考更新DᵀΣₓ^{1/2}中选择子空间，并通过在colspan(B)上施加可微主成分角损失来维持训练过程中的子空间对齐。

Result: 在合成问题上，子空间错位项从51%降至接近0，子空间对齐度从0.49提升至1.00。在RoBERTa-large到RoBERTa-base的六项GLUE任务上，r=4时SAD-LoRA在五项任务中匹配或超越最强谱基线，r=8时在SST-2和CoLA上取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 子空间对齐是提升LoRA蒸馏秩效率的主要组件，系数匹配仅起辅助作用，SAD-LoRA通过显式控制子空间显著优于仅依赖输出梯度的标准KD方法。

Abstract: Distilling a fine-tuned teacher into a LoRA-adapted student is a standard recipe for parameter-efficient compression, but output-level KD does not explicitly control which rank-$r$ weight subspace the adapter occupies. We propose \textbf{SAD-LoRA} (\textbf{S}pectral \textbf{A}lignment \textbf{D}istillation), which selects this subspace from the data-weighted student-space reference update $\DWT\Sigx^{1/2}$ and maintains it during training via a differentiable principal-angle loss on $\colspan(B)$. We show that the data-weighted distillation error decomposes exactly into subspace misalignment, within-subspace coefficient mismatch, and irreducible rank residual; standard KD can affect the first term only indirectly through output gradients. On controlled synthetic problems with a flat teacher spectrum, SAD-LoRA reduces the subspace-misalignment term from $51\%$ to nearly zero and lifts final subspace alignment from $0.49$ to $1.00$. On RoBERTa-large to RoBERTa-base distillation across six GLUE tasks, SAD-LoRA improves rank efficiency: at $r{=}4$, it matches or beats the strongest included spectral baseline on five of six tasks, and at $r{=}8$ it gives the best result on SST-2 and CoLA. Ablations identify subspace alignment as the load-bearing component, while coefficient matching is auxiliary.

</details>


### [254] [On the effectiveness of reward functions in reinforcement learning for confidence calibration of large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04332)
*Chee Heng Tan,Zhuoyi Lin,Mehul Motani,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在使用强化学习训练大型语言模型时，如何同时提升推理准确性和置信度表达能力。作者发现不当的奖励设计会导致"置信度奖励欺骗"现象，并提出"不可欺骗置信度奖励方案"的概念，通过实验展示了不同奖励方案在准确率与校准度之间的权衡，建议将奖励方案作为超参数进行优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在使用强化学习训练LLM时，同时提升推理准确性和置信度表达能力是一个重要但具有挑战性的目标。不当的奖励设计可能导致模型通过故意答错来获得置信度奖励，即"置信度奖励欺骗"现象，亟需系统性的解决方案。

Method: 提出使用两个不同的奖励函数分别针对正确和错误情况下LLM表达的置信度；定义了"不可欺骗置信度奖励方案"的概念及其谱系；将奖励方案作为超参数进行优化。

Result: 实验证明，在实际数据集中，未设计为不可欺骗的奖励方案会出现选择性置信度奖励欺骗；不同数据集和应用场景下，最佳校准-准确率权衡对应的奖励方案不同。

Conclusion: 奖励方案应作为超参数来优化准确率与置信度校准之间的权衡，以适应不同应用需求；不可欺骗的奖励方案设计对于防止奖励欺骗至关重要。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the setting where large language models (LLMs) are trained using reinforcement learning (RL) to simultaneously improve reasoning accuracy and verbalize its confidence. Our reward scheme uses two functions for rewarding confidence verbalized by the LLM: one when the LLM is correct and a different one when the LLM is incorrect. With a poorly designed reward scheme, the LLM may be incentivized to answer incorrectly so that it can be confident that its answer is indeed incorrect, a phenomenon that we call confidence reward hacking. We propose the concept of non-hackable confidence reward schemes and define a spectrum of such reward schemes for RL confidence calibration training in LLMs. We demonstrate that selective confidence reward hacking can occur in practical datasets with reward schemes that are not designed to be non-hackable. We also demonstrate that the reward scheme with the best calibration to accuracy tradeoff depends on the dataset and the application, and propose using the reward scheme as a hyperparameter to optimize the tradeoffs in accordance to what is important for the application. The code of our experiments is available in https://anonymous.4open.science/r/rl-confidence-calibration-9ED4/README.md.

</details>


### [255] [Structure-Specific Representational Priors Causally Control the Grokking Delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04333)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过注入不同结构内容的表征先验，本文因果验证了"延迟泛化"(grokking)现象本质上是模型形成正确任务结构表征所需的时间。真实结构加速泛化，错误但连贯的结构不影响泛化但无加速，随机结构完全破坏泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于grokking延迟是否专门用于形成任务结构化表征仅停留在观察性论断层面，缺乏因果验证。本文旨在通过干预表征结构来因果检验这一假设。

Method: 在单层transformer学习模加法任务中，注入三种不同结构内容的监督对比辅助损失：(i)真实等价结构(a+b) mod p，(ii)错误但连贯的兄弟结构(a-b) mod p，(iii)随机划分。控制损失形式、强度、类别大小和几何形状完全相同。

Result: 真实结构22/30泛化，兄弟结构14/15泛化，随机结构0/20泛化(Fisher精确检验p=1.3×10⁻⁷)。权重范数对照实验0/15泛化。表征探针表明结构形成先于并预测泛化。真实结构加速泛化高达2.75倍，但存在剂量依赖和双峰分布。

Conclusion: Grokking延迟因果上是形成正确表征结构所需的时间，其中"正确"由特征层面而非标签层面决定：错误但连贯的结构不破坏泛化，随机结构完全消除泛化，只有真实结构能加速泛化。

Abstract: Grokking -- generalization arriving long after training-set interpolation -- can be accelerated by structure-agnostic interventions: gradient filtering, weight-norm clamping, geometric penalties on hidden representations. Whether the delay specifically measures the time to form task-structured representations has remained an observational claim. We test it causally by injecting representational priors of varying structural content into a one-layer transformer learning modular addition: a supervised-contrastive auxiliary loss whose positives encode (i) the task's true equivalence structure ($(a+b) \bmod p$), (ii) a coherent-but-wrong sibling structure ($(a-b) \bmod p$), or (iii) a random partition, all with identical loss form, strength, class sizes, and geometry. Whether generalization occurs follows a clean gradation: true structure 22/30 runs; sibling structure, which needs the same periodic features but the wrong combination, 14/15; random partition, satisfiable only by memorization, 0/20 (Fisher exact $p = 1.3 \times 10^{-7}$). A weight-norm-matched control replaying each intervention's norm trajectory onto plain cross-entropy generalizes in 0/15, collapsing into logit-scale saturation, ruling out the norm as mediator. Representation probes show structure formation precedes and predicts generalization in all 95 runs. Only the true structure also accelerates grokking, up to $2.75\times$ faster than baseline, but the acceleration is dose-dependent, bimodal across seeds, and a net wall-clock win only in its strongest cases given the contrastive term's overhead. The grokking delay is, causally, the time to form the right representational structure, where "right" is decided at the level of features rather than labels: coherent-but-wrong structure leaves grokking intact, random structure abolishes it, and only the true structure hastens it.

</details>


### [256] [One Framework for All: Cross-Modal Membership Inference for Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04339)
*Dayong Ye,Tainqing Zhu,Kun Gao,Junhao Liu,Yichuan Chen,Shuai Zhou,Hengzhu Liu,Bo Liu,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个跨文本-文本、文本-图像、图像-文本三种模态的统一成员推理攻击框架，利用生成模型输出分布近似训练数据分布的特性，在共享嵌入空间通过似然比检验进行成员推断，在严格黑盒设置下优于现有针对单一模态的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
